With multiple admissible targets, worst-case regret is near-linear, $\widetildeΘ(m_{\mathrm{eff}})$, in the effective scale given by endpoint-rank compression; a single fixed target, as in standard QA, admits an explicit polynomial-time learner with optimal $Θ(\log m_{\mathrm{eff}})$ regret, run on real NQ episodes. On Natural Questions, containment yields a large deductive gain plus a smaller learned gain on unresolved candidates, reproduced across two backbones and DPR hierarchies. Expected Search-space Reduction (ESR) turns these probabilities into decisions, localizing evidence in fewer verifier calls on SciFact, ERASER, and DPR hierarchies; on DPR hierarchies under call budgets it outperforms four recent external selectors and raises fixed-reader F1 over learned and generator-greedy scheduling.

</details>


### [180] [DAMI: Dual-Axis Memory Initialization for Enhanced Long-Context Retrieval in Gated Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41994)
*Zhangchi Feng,Harry Jiang,Yangyifei Luo,Ray Zhang,Richong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入了一种名为Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI) 的初始化方法，可以在无需增加可训练参数的情况下提高多个注意力网络的检索准确率，特别是在长时序上下文检索中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性注意力机制虽然随着序列长度线性扩展，但其有限状态记忆限制了长上下文的检索。通过实验证明，门控线性递归中的记忆保留依赖于状态衰减，这一过程由门控参数A_log和dt_bias以及输入激活共同决定。因此，需要一种新的初始化方法以优化这些参数，进而改善模型性能。

Method: DAMI方法通过对门控参数A_log和dt_bias及输入激活设置不同的几何时间尺度和低-高-低的输入敏感性分布，进行了训练前的初始化设置。并且通过一个小的激活样本校准GDN家族实施，无需增加额外的可训练参数。

Result: 在包括GDN、KDA、GDN2、Mamba-2和RWKV-7在内的注意力网络模型中，DAMI方法平均提高了5.29到16.71个百分点的检索准确率。

Conclusion: DAMI方法通过优化门控参数和输入激活的配置，在保证语言建模、常识推理和实际检索等任务性能基本与基线模型相当的情况下，大幅提升了基于GDN家族的注意力网络的检索准确率，证明了其在长时序上下文检索中的优越性。

Abstract: Linear attention scales linearly with sequence length, but its finite-state memory can limit long-context retrieval. In gated linear recurrences, memory retention depends on state decay, which is shaped jointly by gate parameters A_log and dt_bias and by input activations. In controlled Gated DeltaNet (GDN) experiments, both parameters remain highly correlated with their initial values after training, highlighting the importance of initialization. Therefore, we introduce Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI), which jointly configures these parameters before training. DAMI assigns geometric timescales across heads for varied retrieval distances and a low-high-low input-sensitivity profile across layers. A small activation sample calibrates the GDN-family implementation to match both behavioral targets without adding trainable parameters. Across GDN, KDA, GDN2, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7, DAMI improves average RULER-style retrieval accuracy by 5.29 to 16.71 percentage points over the corresponding random-initialization baselines. Performance on language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and 2K real-world retrieval remains broadly comparable to those baselines. Our code is available at https://speedai.com/research/dami.

</details>


### [181] [Cross-Interface Authorization for AI Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41397)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于配置的协议，针对固定版本的计算机使用部署。该协议列出具有影响的操作路径和持久存储目标，检查路径到目标的执行切割，将每个具体请求转换为统一的效应，颁发路径绑定的预备票，并在提交时验证路径、效应、授权、追踪、配置文件、库存和权威状态。在明确假设下，论文证明了基于配置的细化、匹配效应决策不变性、抵抗替代路径洗钱和预宣跨面重新组合、提交时间状态安全性以及基于收据的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机在于确保在图形界面、命令行、API、脚本及模型上下文协议服务器等不同接口下的计算机使用环境中的政策执行的一致性，避免潜在的安全隐患如绕过中介进行篡改或提交超出授权状态的行为。

Method: 该方法通过列举具有影响的操作路径和持久化目标，检查路径到目标的执行裁决，将每个具体请求标准化为统一效应，颁发路径绑定的预备票，并在提交时验证路径、效应、授权、追踪、配置文件、库存和权威状态。

Result: 研究结果验证了该协议能够应对配置基的细化、匹配效应决策不变性、抵抗替代路径洗钱及预宣跨面重新组合的能力，并确保提交时间状态安全及基于收据的结果。此外，测试展示了不同结构下的运行时映射产生了大量验证通过的踪迹和场景。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在计算机使用策略的一致执行和安全性方面取得了重要进展，确保了网络安全并防止了潜在的安全风险。

Abstract: Computer-use agents act through graphical interfaces, command lines, APIs, scripts, and Model Context Protocol servers. A policy decision can be interface-invariant while its executable authorization is not: another route can bypass mediation, alter a security-relevant default, or commit after the authorized state has changed. We present a profile-bounded protocol for a fixed, versioned computer-use deployment. It enumerates effect-bearing routes and durable sinks, checks a route-to-sink enforcement cut, normalizes each concrete request to a canonical effect, issues a route-bound prepare ticket, and verifies the route, effect, grant, trace, profile, inventory, and authoritative state again at commit. A committed result requires an agreeing sink receipt. Under explicit assumptions, we prove profile-bounded refinement, matched-effect decision invariance, resistance to alternate-route laundering and declared cross-surface recomposition, commit-time state safety, and receipt-grounded outcomes.
  The reference profile registers 12 effect families, five interfaces, and 60 routes. Two structurally distinct runtime mappings produce 3,464 traces over 1,732 scenarios each: 960 commits, 2,312 denials, and 192 commits after re-preparation. An independent checker accepts every retained trace and pair and rejects 26/26 semantic regressions; coverage and normalizer suites reject 24/24 and 720/720 mutants; and 168/168 commit probes match their declared outcomes. A frozen-source audit passes 52 checks over 12 files from two independent hybrid runtimes and one public authorization substrate. Source-shaped adapter refinements commit 48/48 instrumented calls and reject 15/15 unbound cases. The resulting guarantees bind the declared profile and its effect semantics.

</details>


### [182] [The Knowledge Layer Model (KLM): Evidence-Visible AI Output, Routing Diagnostics, and Negative Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42066)
*Omer Asim Ozturk,Cihan Aydiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究引入了知识层模型(KLM)，一个用于推理记录的开放性、实施中立的规范。SEDIM是Nage AI OS的知识与证据架构，KLM v0.3已实现。研究中的实验数据有限，分类准确率报告未经全面验证，只有一些局部诊断结果。未来工作旨在改进验证和全面公审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统对答案的正确性评价过于表面化，需要一种更深入的检查机制。知识层模型和其实施(SEDIM)旨在提供一种全面的推理记录规范，以供审查其知识基础。

Method: 研究者引入了KLM用于描述推理记录，并通过不同实验和验证措施检验其有效性。包括分类实验、模型配置实验、诊断实验等。

Result: 研究结果显示某些实验取得了部分成功，如部分分类准确率高，但其他局部诊断结果较差，原始数据和部分配置未能进行有效验证。此外，历史平台测试环境存在缺陷，修复后的包装操作保留了关键信息。

Conclusion: 尽管KLM在概念上是有效的，但仍需进一步的独立验证和全面的公共审计来证明其在实际应用中的效果。未来的工作将集中在增加实验数据支持、改进验证方法等方面。

Abstract: AI answers require more than apparent correctness: reviewers need to inspect their recorded basis. We introduce the Knowledge Layer Model (KLM), an open, implementation-neutral specification for inference records with versioned epistemic assessments, evidence relations, and explicit unknowns. SEDIM, the knowledge and evidence architecture of Nage AI OS, is a reference implementation. We reference KLM v0.3 (repository tag v0.3.0). Empirical material consists of separate internal experiments, not end-to-end validation. A historical platform-domain routing benchmark reports 137/150 classifications across 15 classes (91.3%; nominal 95% Wilson interval 85.7-94.9%); the numerator is recovered from a rounded summary, and historical example membership is unverified. A native-model weight-path configuration yielded 0/15 successful answers in a corrected two-source test. A capacity-growth comparison could not establish a reliable effect because of a defective legacy scorer and missing original outputs. Lexical and scoped diagnostics each yielded AUC 0.008 for silent-versus-contradicted examples, below the registered minimum of 0.65. Legacy packing lost gold-containing text in 118/221 selected cases; repaired packing retained it in 221/221. These measure text presence, not answer quality. Python and TypeScript verifiers reportedly agreed on six checks per synthetic fixture; a separate production disclosure was not accepted. Restoration, re-derivation, human-rubric support, boundary isolation, and bounded-response evaluations remain proposed. Findings are internal and author-reported; historical observations are distinguished from later reproductions. Evidence visibility does not establish correctness, causal reliance, security, or regulatory compliance. Independent replication and a complete public audit package remain future work.

</details>


### [183] [scAnyOmics: Foundation-Model Distillation for Any-to-Any Completion of Multi-omics Virtual Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41399)
*Zhaoqi Song,Ye Du,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qian Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: scAnyOmics 是一种生成框架，能够从冷冻的单细胞基础模型中提取多模态知识，并将其整合到学生模型中，实现跨模态预测与缺失模态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前单细胞技术能够同时测序多种分子模态，但如何集成和预测缺失的分子信息仍具有挑战性，尤其是在配对测量受限的情况下。

Method: scAnyOmics 使用一种生成框架，将冷冻的单细胞基础模型中的知识传递到多模态学生模型中，将 scRNA-seq、蛋白标记和 scATAC-seq 转换为共享表示形式，从而实现跨模态预测和重建。

Result: 在六项公开研究中收集的 690,880 个细胞数据集上系统性地评估其预测性能，结果表明在多个评估指标上优于基线方法。此外，scAnyOmics 还在未见过的数据集上进行了细胞类型注释、扰动响应预测和生物年龄估计等方面的应用研究，并取得了良好的性能。

Conclusion: scAnyOmics 提供了一个统一框架，用于整合不完整的单细胞多组学数据并将预训练的生物学知识传授给模型，支持虚拟细胞模型的构建，适用于多种细胞和实验平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in single-cell technologies have enabled the profiling of multiple molecular modalities, including transcriptomics, proteomics, and chromatin accessibility, providing a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states. However, integrating heterogeneous modalities and predicting missing molecular information remain challenging, particularly when paired measurements are limited. Here, we introduce scAnyOmics, a generative framework that distills knowledge from a frozen single-cell foundation model into a multimodal student model. scAnyOmics integrates scRNA-seq, protein-marker measurements, and scATAC-seq into a shared representation, enabling cross-modal prediction and reconstruction of missing modalities. We assembled 690,880 cells from six publicly available studies, covering both paired and partially paired multi-omics measurements, to systematically evaluate its predictive performance. We further evaluated scAnyOmics on held-out donor data, where it outperformed baseline methods across multiple evaluation metrics. Beyond cross-modal reconstruction, we explored its applications in cell-type annotation, perturbation-response prediction, and biological age estimation. Overall, scAnyOmics provides a unified framework for integrating incomplete single-cell multi-omics data and transferring pretrained biological knowledge, supporting the development of virtual cell models across diverse cellular contexts and experimental platforms.

</details>


### [184] [CryoAtlas: A Large Curated Dataset and Unified Benchmark for Cryo-EM Atomic Model Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41411)
*Mingrui Li,Minzhang Li,Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Zhangzhi Xiong,Sixian Shen,Jiakai Zhang,Yuan Pei,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CryoAtlas 提供了一个高质量且标准化的原子模型构建数据集和基准，它整合和过滤了 EMDB 和 PDB 数据，用于蛋白质链的密度导向原子模型构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前，自动构建原子模型受限于数据集规模不足及缺乏标准化评估基准，CryoAtlas 致力于解决这一问题。

Method: 通过集成并过滤 EMDB 和 PDB 数据（直至 2026 年 3 月），CryoAtlas 提供了包含 17,809 个模型-地图-序列条目的数据集，并从中构建了一个包含 618 个目标的基准测试。

Result: CryoAtlas 使用共同的评估协议评估了最先进的基于学习的方法，并重新训练了 ModelAngelo 架构以提高基准测试中的性能。

Conclusion: CryoAtlas 作为一个开放、可重复的基础平台，为未来的冷冻电镜原子模型构建研究提供了资源。

Abstract: Deep learning has accelerated automated atomic model building from cryo-EM maps, yet progress remains limited by the lack of large, high-quality datasets and standardized evaluation benchmarks. We address this gap by introducing CryoAtlas, a rigorously curated dataset and unified benchmark for density-guided atomic model building of protein chains. By integrating and filtering EMDB and PDB entries (up to March 2026), CryoAtlas provides 17,809 map-model-sequence entries, including a curated 16,657-entry training split. From this resource, we construct a 618-target benchmark with a shared evaluation protocol that jointly assesses structural accuracy, model-to-map fit, and stereochemical validity. Using this framework, we benchmark state-of-the-art learning-based methods against Phenix and AlphaFold3, comprehensively analyzing the impact of map resolution, sequence length, protein category and sequence identity on modeling accuracy. As a case study, retraining the ModelAngelo CNN component on CryoAtlas improves performance on the benchmark. CryoAtlas provides an open, reproducible foundation for future cryo-EM atomic model building research. Project details and resource release updates are available at https://oulling.github.io/cryoatlas/.

</details>


### [185] [Evidence-Guided Recovery for AI Agents with Ambiguous Tool Outcomes and Partial Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41453)
*Genliang Zhu,Xiaobo Chen,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种工具超时情景下的任务恢复方法，通过证据引导，考虑了任务的完成与安全完成之间的区别，提供了模型验证结果，表明与证据提示相比，任务建议在认证完成任务方面更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决AI代理在面对工具超时导致的任务执行不确定性和部分完成时的恢复问题，提出了证据引导的任务恢复方法。

Method: 该方法通过标准观测条件下的规划和单独实施的效果历史裁判员相结合，在有限的研究中验证了其实现。

Result: 在37项任务的研究中，规划和其他三个主动对照组各自认证了28次完成；四次合成交易费产生额外计算；在58对之前开发的任务系列的58个单元中，建议认证了30次完成，比证据提示多出309次。

Conclusion: 该方法在任务恢复中比证据提示更有效，所有路径都通过了独立的回放测试。

Abstract: Tool timeouts can leave AI agents facing ambiguous tool outcomes and partial execution: an action may have failed, committed or remained in flight while other workflow steps have already completed. We formulate evidence-guided task recovery over approved obligations, provider capabilities and states compatible with public evidence. A shared admissibility check handles key expiry, authorization timing, dependencies and residual effects; a terminal certificate separates actual goal satisfaction from publicly supported safe completion. The known-model implementation combines standard observation-contingent planning with a separately implemented effect-history adjudicator. In a finite study of 37 assignments, planning and three active controls each certify 28 completions; four saved synthetic fee units incur additional computation. A preregistered assignment-held-out comparison then evaluates planning advice against evidence prompting with two local model bundles. Across 58 paired units in 19 previously developed task families, advice certifies 30 completions versus 9, a 36.2-percentage-point difference (exact family sign-flip p=0.0078), using 272 proposals versus 399. All 116 trajectories pass independent replay. Both models exhibit a positive completion difference. The comparison measures the evaluated advice interface under a common shield; search-versus-rule advice requires a separate matched comparison. Unknown and handoff outcomes remain explicit, with guarantees tied to the declared provider capabilities rather than arbitrary third-party exactly-once execution.

</details>


### [186] [EvoMCP: Co-evolving MCP agents and training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41479)
*Xuanang Gao,Kepeng Lin,Xinyan Shi,Yong Deng,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Xiaofeng Wu,Jie Yang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为\method的框架，通过任务和轨迹的共同进化，实现了MCP代理的自我改进。该框架首先从学生的反馈中评估其能力和差距，然后根据这些信息调整任务指令和环境，再利用可重用技能改进演示，最后通过反馈闭合循环，完善学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的监督微调仅适用于静态数据分布，无法适应学生不断进阶的能力并解决新出现的弱点，因此需要一种能自我进化的框架。

Method: 该方法包括任务进化、轨迹进化和监督微调三个步骤。首先，通过执行反馈评估学生的能力和差距；接着，调整任务指令和环境，确保匹配验证标准的任务；然后，使用重用的技能来改进演示，并将新任务输入用于引导数据进化的循环。

Result: 在Toolathlon，MCPMark，和MCP-Atlas上，该方法实现了相对于初始模型16.5%，10.2%，和12.6%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了该框架的有效性，是解决MCP代理适应能力动态变化的有效方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of Model Context Protocol (MCP) agents is typically conducted on a static data distribution, which fails to adapt to the student's evolving capabilities and leaves newly exposed weaknesses insufficiently addressed. To address these challenges, we propose \method, an agent--data mutual evolution framework that enables MCP agents to improve through a closed loop of task evolution, trajectory evolution, and supervised fine-tuning. Specifically, \method\ assesses the student's established capabilities and capability gaps from rollout feedback to identify learning targets. Task evolution then translates these targets into executable tasks by adapting the instructions and observable environment states of compatible verified seed tasks while keeping their verifiers aligned. To provide reliable and efficient demonstrations for these tasks, trajectory evolution uses reusable skills to recover failed teacher rollouts and refine successful ones. The resulting task--trajectory pairs are used to fine-tune the student, while accepted tasks seed subsequent evolution. To close the loop, we re-evaluate the updated student and use its feedback to guide the next round of data evolution. Experiments on Toolathlon, MCPMark, and MCP-Atlas demonstrate consistent improvements over the initial student, yielding absolute gains of 16.5\%, 10.2\%, and 12.6\%, respectively. Project page: https://github.com/xagao/EvoMCP.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-Test Nation Bias Benchmarking of LLMs with Explicit Unbiased Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41486)
*Jonghyeon Choi,Yeonjun Choi,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过扩展语料库和多测试方法，量化了联合国安理会记录中的国别偏见，发现模型输出在对待中国和俄罗斯时偏向于反对派，在对待英美法时则偏向于支持派，强调了跨测试基准审计面对国际关系的LLM部署的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估大型语言模型中国家偏向的方法缺乏明确的无偏参考，研究使用722份官方联合国安理会记录，提出了五种互补测试方法，以此填补这一空白。

Method: 研究采用了包含意见排名、关联排序、投票模拟、反对归因和框架构建在内的多测试方法，以此测量在联合国安理会记录中的国别偏见，特别为意见排名和关联排序提出了基于行动的伪无偏基准。

Result: 研究发现，在对待中国和俄罗斯时，模型输出偏向于反对派；而在对待英美法时，则偏向于支持派。这表明需要使用跨测试基准结合明确的参考点来审查面对国际关系的LLM部署。

Conclusion: 研究强调了跨测试基准审计和结合明确参考点对于审查和改进面对国际关系的LLM部署的重要性。

Abstract: While prior work has highlighted that systematic nation-level bias in large language models (LLMs) can pose operational risks for international relations (IR) applications, many existing evaluations still lack a clearly specified unbiased reference (ground truth), limiting fully quantitative and cross-setting measurement. Using an expanded corpus of 722 official UN Security Council (UNSC) records, we measure nation-level bias with five complementary tests, thereby extending prior approaches to cover a broader set of bias expression channels: opinion ranking (WOW), association sorting (AK+-), vote simulation (PVS), opposition attribution (F-Oppose), and framing (Headliner). To enable grounded bias evaluation, we explicitly define an unbiased status for each test; in particular, for WOW and AK+- where ground truth is difficult to specify, we propose an action-based pseudo unbiased status derived from observable voting behavior to construct a quantifiable baseline. Benchmarking 4 LLMs (GPT-4o-mini, Llama 3.3-70B, Mistral-22B-Small, and Qwen3-30B) across the five permanent members of the UNSC, we find a test-agnostic macro-pattern: relative to the reference baselines defined for each test, model outputs lean toward the opposition (red) side for China and Russia and toward the supportive (blue) side for the U.K., the U.S., and France. These results underscore the need to audit IR-facing LLM deployments with grounded, multi-test benchmarks tied to explicit reference points.

</details>


### [188] [Specification-Guided Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41511)
*Alessandro Previti,Konstantinos Vandikas,Mario Alviano,Mats Carlsson,Albin Larsson Forsberg,Alexandros Nikou,Athanasios Karapantelakis,Aneta Vulgarakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种神经符号、规范引导的解码框架，用于提高大型语言模型生成满足正式规范的结构化输出的能力。该框架结合了LLM生成和符号推理，通过过滤无法扩展到满足规范的输出来精炼解码语法规则。实验证明，该框架能够减少重复的LLM推理，提高模型对提示的解释一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受限解码方法主要关注语法约束，而不能直接保证满足正式规范。因此，引入一种结合LLM生成和符号推理的方法，以增强生成满足规范的结构化输出的能力。

Method: 该框架采用神经符号方法，通过在每个步骤中精炼解码语法规则来过滤掉无法扩展到满足规范的输出。它包括一种前进式变体，该变体通过构造确保规范满足，并且还有一种回溯式变体，可以在后续选择导致LSTM自身判断为不合适时进行修正。

Result: 在配置和约束求解基准测试上，该框架的前进式变体能够可靠地生成符合规范的输出，并且减少了与拒绝采样相比重复的LLM推理，同时只有一小部分涉及符号推理解释。回溯式变体进一步降低了模型在自身判断不合适的情况下选择参数的情况，显著提高了模型对提示的解释一致性。

Conclusion: 神经符号、规范引导的解码框架能够提高大型语言模型生成满足正式规范的结构化输出的能力，尤其在提高对LSTM自身解释的一致性方面表现出色。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate structured outputs, but existing constrained-decoding methods mainly enforce syntax and cannot directly enforce satisfaction of a formal specification. We introduce a neuro-symbolic, specification-guided decoding framework that combines LLM generation with symbolic reasoning. At each step, the framework refines the decoding grammar by filtering out values that cannot be extended to an output satisfying the specification. We present a forward-only variant that guarantees specification satisfaction by construction, and a backtracking variant that revises earlier feasible choices when their downstream consequences leave only values that the LLM itself judges unsuitable. The framework is model-agnostic, requires no retraining, and builds on existing grammar-constrained decoding mechanisms. Experiments on configuration and constraint-solving benchmarks show that the forward-only variant reliably produces specification-compliant outputs while reducing repeated LLM inference compared with rejection sampling and incurring modest symbolic-reasoning overhead. The backtracking variant further reduces cases in which the model is forced to select values that it has itself judged unsuitable, thereby substantially improving adherence to the LLM's stated interpretation of the prompt.

</details>


### [189] [Verify--Backtrack Loop: Structured Precondition Recovery for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41564)
*Mingyang Liu,Qingcan Kang,Shixiong Kai,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Xin Li,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VBL（验证回退循环）通过任务分解为有界目标堆栈，并在目标激活、执行失败或物料状态变化时对两个时间尺度进行验证，改进了ReAct和其他反应式控制方法，特别是在AppWorld复杂场景中，成功提高了任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的反应式代理在处理长周期任务时遇到的主要挑战在于，在前提缺失或早期分解失败的情况下，尝试其他可能的动作往往无效。为了解决这个问题，作者提出了VBL（验证回退循环），旨在通过结构化的规划状态更新来实现前提发现和失败诊断，从而改善长期任务的执行效果。

Method: VBL引入了有界目标堆栈，并在任务目标激活、执行失败或物料状态变化时验证目标是否对终目标有贡献及是否在当前状态下可行，通过类型化的失败归因机制触发推入、弹出、重试或回退至父任务等显式操作。

Result: 该方法在AppWorld、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld等环境中与Qwen3.5-9B、GPT-4o、DeepSeek-V3.2等模型结合测试，表现出优于ReAct和基于反应式控制的方法，特别是在AppWorld的复杂场景中，能显著提升任务完成率。

Conclusion: VBL显示了对规划状态的修订对可靠长周期互动的重要性，而不仅仅是对下一个动作的修订。删除验证或诊断都会降低性能，证实这两个机制是互补的。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks in interactive environments, where actions often depend on latent preconditions. The core difficulty is not trajectory length but recovery: when a prerequisite is missing or an earlier decomposition fails, trying another plausible action rarely helps. ReAct and later reflection or adaptive-planning methods support local correction, but express recovery as free-form text, with no explicit control state linking the terminal goal, the active goal, and the failure source. We introduce the Verify--Backtrack Loop (VBL), a training-free extension of ReAct that turns precondition discovery and failure diagnosis into structured planning-state updates. VBL maintains the task decomposition as a bounded goal stack and, on goal activation, execution failure, or material state change, jointly verifies two horizons---whether the active goal contributes to the terminal goal and whether it is feasible in the current state. These dual-horizon judgments, combined with typed failure attribution, trigger explicit operations---Push, Pop, retry, or BacktrackTo the parent. We evaluate VBL with Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-V3.2 on AppWorld, ALFWorld, and ScienceWorld. VBL improves over ReAct and reflection-based control on every backbone--benchmark pair. On the challenging AppWorld setting, it improves task success by up to 22.07 percentage points over ReAct. Ablations show that removing either verification or diagnosis degrades performance, in several settings below ReAct, confirming that the two mechanisms are complementary. These results show that reliable long-horizon interaction benefits from revising the planning state, not merely the next action.

</details>


### [190] [Connectivity-Aware Client Selection in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41570)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Monica Nicoli,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了利用谱图工具进行轻量级客户端选择的方法，通过分析图拉普拉斯的谱特性来指导客户端选择最相关的邻居，从而提高去中心化联邦学习的效率和准确性，同时减少了沟通消息的数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化联邦学习方法依赖于全邻域广播，导致高通信开销和能耗。本文的目标是通过利用图拓扑和谱图工具来减轻这一问题，改进客户端的选择机制。

Method: 通过分析固定通信图上的图拉普拉斯谱特性，提出了基于特征向量嵌入和热/扩散核相似性的客户端邻域选择方法，旨在减少通信成本并提高模型收敛速度。

Result: 该方法在具有高数据异质性的大型结构丰富图上表现突出，准确率接近全广播模式，同时通信消息总数减少了50％。

Conclusion: 相对于均匀邻域抽样，这种拓扑感知随机化更优越或至少等效，表明这种方法可以有效实现去中心化联邦学习的性能优化。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) has emerged as a scalable alternative to centralized federated learning, removing the single point of failure and communication bottleneck at the server. However, most existing decentralized approaches rely on full-neighborhood broadcasting, where each client transmits its model update to all of its neighbors in each communication round. While effective, this results in significant communication overhead and high energy consumption. This paper applies spectral graph tools as lightweight client selection methods that leverage the graph topology by analyzing the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian over a fixed communication graph. Specifically, we exploit eigenvector embeddings and heat/diffusion kernel similarities to guide each client in selecting only a subset of structurally relevant neighbors, i.e., those that are most beneficial for the convergence of the federation process. This topology aware randomization matches or outperforms uniform neighborhood subsampling and approaches the accuracy of full broadcasting. The largest gains, about $3$ percentage points, emerge under strong data heterogeneity on larger, structurally rich graphs, while the total number of transmitted messages is reduced by $50\%$ compared to naive broadcasting.

</details>


### [191] [Patch-Based Deep Kernel Learning for Data Assimilation and Bias Correction with Extremely Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41596)
*Emanuele Mele,Angelo Coluccia,Massimo Cafaro,Italo Epicoco,Paola Nassisi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合卷积神经网络（CNN）和可学习内核高斯过程（GP）的混合框架，用于利用稀疏观测数据进行数据同化和偏差校正。此方法在保持高精度的同时，显著降低了推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据同化方法如3D-Var在处理大尺度估计算法时存在背景误差协方差的限制，无法捕捉到状态相关的相关性和系统模型偏差。同时，这些方法的成本随着域尺寸的增加而增加。传统的DL方法虽然可以在非线性下执行数据同化和偏差校正，但通常解释性和不确定性估计能力有限。该研究旨在提出一种新的方法，结合CNN和可学习内核GP，以更准确地利用稀疏观测数据进行同化和偏差修正。

Method: 该方法提出了一种混合框架，结合使用了CNN和可学习内核GP，旨在从稀疏观测数据中进行数据同化和偏差校正。模型通过最大化边缘对数似然来端到端训练，学习一个基于数据的网格高斯过程协方差，并提供最大后验分析和误差估计。提供了一种基于块的策略来降低训练成本，并确保推理依然精确。

Result: 在海表温度和盐度实验中，该框架在与传统的3D-Var、经典GP和竞争DL模型相比时，表现出更高的性能，并且在与3D-Var相比时将推理时间减少了超过98%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法为在实际应用中使用稀疏观测数据进行数据同化和偏差校正提供了一种高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Data Assimilation (DA) reconstructs the state of a spatio-temporal system by combining sensor observations with the output of a numerical model. Since model states are high-dimensional while observations cover only a small fraction of the domain, DA is a challenging large-scale estimation problem, central to weather forecasting and oceanography. Classical variational methods such as 3D-Var rely on a hand-crafted background error covariance that assumes unbiased errors, and therefore cannot capture state-dependent correlations or systematic model biases. Moreover, their cost grows with the size of the domain. Deep learning (DL) methods can perform DA and bias correction (BC) non-linearly, but typically offer limited interpretability and no calibrated uncertainty estimates. We propose a hybrid framework that couples a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a learnable-kernel Gaussian Process (GP) to jointly perform DA and BC from extremely sparse observations. The model is trained end-to-end by maximizing the marginal log-likelihood, learns a data-driven covariance over the whole grid, and provides both the Maximum A Posteriori analysis and its uncertainty by design. A patch-based strategy reduces the $O(N^3)$ training cost of exact GPs over $N$ grid points to $O(N)$ for a fixed patch size. Inference remains exact and only requires solving a linear system whose $O(m^3)$ cost depends on the number of observations $m \ll N$, not on the domain. The learned covariance can also replace the hand-crafted one in 3D-Var, improving its accuracy without changing the variational formulation. Experiments on sea surface temperature and salinity, with both synthetic and real, extremely sparse in-situ observations, show that the framework outperforms 3D-Var, a classical GP and a competing DL model, while reducing inference time by more than 98% with respect to 3D-Var.

</details>


### [192] [Real Computation or Merely Naming? A Pre-Registered Mechanism Audit of 25 Cognitive-Architecture Modules in an LLM-Based Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41604)
*Peter Chen,Ni Zhang,Ying Xu,Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种验证基于本地大语言模型的代理系统中认知机制的方法，通过4个问题的静态审计协议、代码行号证据和调用链追踪，对模块进行审计。结果表明，25个主张的认知机制中有18个从未产生生产输出，53个预注册的谬误标准中有53个被确认，修复实验提高了规划任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前基于大语言模型的代理系统在宣称具有广泛认知机制时可能存在虚假或夸张问题，迫切需要一个可验证的方法来检测这些机制是否真正存在。

Method: 研究提出了一个包含四个问题的静态审计协议，使用代码行号和调用链追踪来验证系统中的认知机制。

Result: 通过该协议，研究团队审计了两个代理系统，确认了25个机制中有18个从未产生生产输出，53个谬误标准中有53个被确认。
其中，三个新形态的代码结构被发现，即与未调用代码相连、被调用但不活跃和被调用但内容盲的形态。干预测试后，验证了53个标准中的53个，两个部分确定，五项被反驳，一项未确定。修复实验提高了规划任务的成功率，减少了状态控制器的错误。

Conclusion: 研究证明模块名称和模块计算必须作为独立的声明进行审查，所提议的方法和五类失败分类可以用于该问题的多种系统，对代理系统中认知机制的评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: Agent systems built on local large language models are increasingly marketed through the vocabulary of cognitive architecture: modules named global workspace, theory of mind, causal reasoning, or active inference are listed as features, and the presence of the name is taken as the presence of the mechanism. We ask the prior question -- does the named computation actually happen -- and contribute a transferable verification method for the claim-implementation gap: a four-question static audit protocol with line-number evidence and call-chain tracing, demonstrated on two systems. We audit a system claiming 25 cognitive mechanisms, pre-register falsifiable predictions for 11 modules and execute the interventions, replicate the protocol on the IIT-phi core of a second, independent system with its own pre-committed falsification criteria, and run two repair experiments as positive controls. In 18 of 25 modules the computed value never reaches any production output; three idle-wiring shapes new to the dead-code and code-smell literatures emerge (connected-but-never-called, called-but-inert, called-but-content-blind). Of 61 frozen criteria across 12 interventions, 53 were confirmed, two partially adjudicated, five falsified, one adjudication-ambiguous. Repairs raised planner task success from 0.028 to 1.000 and cut state-controller error by 52%. Module names and module computations must be audited as independent claims; the protocol and the five-class failure taxonomy are reusable for the claim-implementation gap at two-system, case-level depth.

</details>


### [193] [What Should LLMs Forget: Reinforcement Unlearning of Knowledge Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41616)
*Wutong Xu,Yun Qu,Haiyan Feng,Qi Wang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为KARU的框架，通过知识关联(KA)表征查询上下文与目标知识之间的关联，并利用该框架抑制被遗忘的知识关联，同时保持保留的知识关联，从而实现了更精细的知识去识别与保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM去识别方法通常优化整个响应，而未明确标识需要遗忘的知识，导致遗忘过程不精确并且没有控制。本文重新审视LLM去识别的知识目标，并提出了基于知识关联(KA)的去识别方法，旨在实现更有效的遗忘。

Method: KARU框架基于知识关联表征方法，通过构建标记级奖励、分布级正则化和临近感知保留抽样等机制，实现在去除特定知识的同时保持模型的一般功能。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验表明，与其他现有基线方法相比，KARU方法在遗忘效果上表现出更强的优势，同时保持了相似的保留性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: KARU方法通过精确的遗忘控制和保留策略，为LLM的去识别提供了新的方法，有望促进该领域的发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire extensive factual knowledge during pre-training, which may include sensitive, private, or copyrighted information, motivating the development of unlearning methods that selectively remove specific knowledge while preserving general capabilities. However, existing approaches typically optimize entire responses without explicitly identifying the knowledge to be forgotten, resulting in coarse and uncontrolled forgetting. To address this, we revisit the forgetting target of LLM unlearning and introduce Knowledge Association (KA), which characterizes the association between a query context and its target knowledge and is instantiated by the corresponding conditional predictive distribution, as the fundamental target of forgetting. Based on KA, we propose Knowledge Association Reinforcement Unlearning (KARU), a unified reinforcement learning framework that suppresses forgotten knowledge associations while preserving retained ones. Specifically, KARU derives complementary mechanisms from the KA-based formulation: token-level reward construction for finer-grained forgetting supervision, distribution-level regularization for precise knowledge suppression, and proximity-aware retain sampling to mitigate collateral degradation. Experiments across diverse LLMs on representative benchmarks demonstrate that KARU consistently improves forgetting effectiveness over existing baselines while maintaining comparable retention performance and general utility.

</details>


### [194] [Chance-Constrained Inspection Viewpoint Sequencing for On-Orbit Servicing via Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41639)
*Chloé Carer,Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过将视点选择建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），并运用带拉格朗日松弛的近端策略优化（PPO）解决该问题，实现了在考虑成本、安全和覆盖限制的情况下，为目标卫星选择最佳观察视点。通过将覆盖风险和Δv预算作为明确的约束条件，并使用机会约束来适应导航过程中的不确定性，本文方法在各种风险预算下都能保持在限值内，显著降低了碰撞风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对目标卫星进行在轨服务时，自主检查通常开始于近距离观测，需要精确地选择视角以确保全面覆盖、燃料使用合理且安全。本文通过建模视角选择为约束马尔可夫决策过程，并结合概率策略优化和拉格朗日松弛等方法，有效解决了这一复杂问题。

Method: 本文采用的方法是将视点选择问题建模为CMDP，并利用PPO与拉格朗日松弛结合的方法进行求解。其中，高阶的规划器负责安排相机视点在目标卫星周围观察球面上的次序，而低层次的模型则基于Clohessy--Wiltshire方程评估转移路径中的细节。通过直接在动作集中施加限制条件，确保单次推进可达性和Δv预算限制。此外，通过引入机会约束并在训练过程中使用PID控制来调整乘数，以适应由导航和机动误差引起的实际航路结果的不确定性。

Result: 该方法在不同风险预算下表现良好，能够维持不超出预设违反率的值，接近最大覆盖范围，并在其任务Δv预算之内；相较于现有的方法，它的碰撞风险更低，且无需在每次决策点重复进行蒙特卡洛风险评估。

Conclusion: 文中提出的方法能够有效处理在近距离观测目标卫星过程中所涉及的复杂约束问题，不仅减少了碰撞风险，还提高了任务效率。通过使用机会约束，本文方法能够将碰撞风险作为设计参数，从而减少针对不同情况的重新调节需求。

Abstract: Autonomous inspection of a target spacecraft is typically the initial step in on-orbit servicing. Planning close-proximity observation viewpoints involves a complex combinatorial problem constrained by coverage, fuel, and safety considerations. This paper models the viewpoint selection as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) and applies Proximal Policy Optimization with Lagrangian relaxation to solve it. The system combines a high-level planner, which sequences camera viewpoints on an observation sphere around the target, with a low-level model that assesses each transfer using the Clohessy--Wiltshire (CW) equations for relative motion. The deterministic constraints on single-impulse reachability and $Δv$ cap are enforced directly on the action set. Safety margins are affected by navigation and manoeuvre errors, making the actual clearance stochastic. To address this, a chance constraint on the realised trajectory is implemented, with a Lagrangian multiplier governed by PID control to adapt the penalty during training. The approach is tested on a Galileo-class navigation satellite in medium Earth orbit, modelled as a point cloud where visibility from each viewpoint considers field-of-view and line-of-sight occlusion. Across various risk budgets, the trained policy maintains the keep-out violation rate within the specified limit, achieving coverage close to its maximum and staying within the mission $Δv$ budget. Compared to the usual practice of applying a fixed margin to the keep-out radius and planning deterministically, the chance-constrained policy attains less than half the violation rate at comparable coverage. This makes the collision risk an explicit design parameter, eliminating the need for re-tuning per case. The learned policy selects a viewpoint without the repeated Monte Carlo risk estimates required by a greedy, chance-constrained planner at every decision point.

</details>


### [195] [Risk-Sensitive Mission Planning for Multi-Target On-Orbit Inspection via Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41647)
*Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布强化学习（Distributional Reinforcement Learning）的自适应多任务在轨服务方法，应用于低轨道检查任务，通过量化回归深度Q网络（Quantile-Regression Deep Q-Network）评估目标返回的期望与方差，以支持风险敏感决策，实验结果表明风险厌恶策略在降低回报方差和减少任务失败概率方面优于无风险策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标在轨服务任务和低轨道检查需要适应不同类型的任务和环境变化，同时考虑安全相关的结果变异性，本文的动机是提出一种自适应的方法，能够有效规划这些任务，实现更高水平的任务鲁棒性和效率。

Method: 该方法基于分布强化学习，并使用量化回归深度Q网络（QR-DQN）模拟agent的行为，通过考虑返回值的期望和方差作出决策。

Result: 在五颗目标卫星的场景中进行了数值实验，结果表明，当回报方差相同时，学习到的策略接近效率前沿，风险厌恶策略比非风险策略具有更小的任务失败概率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法能够适应在轨服务任务中不同的运营条件，具体应用于低轨道检查任务，实现了对风险的不同敏感度，通过数值实验验证了其有效性和优势。

Abstract: Multi-target on-orbit servicing missions require planning tools that can adapt to evolving operational conditions while accounting for the outcome variability inherent to safety-critical spaceflight. This paper presents an adaptive planning approach for multi-target servicing campaigns in low Earth orbit and applies it to inspection missions flown by a low-thrust servicer. The approach is based on Distributional Reinforcement Learning, which learns the full distribution of mission returns and thereby supports explicit risk-sensitive decision-making at each planning step. Specifically, a Quantile-Regression Deep Q-Network (QR-DQN) agent selects which target to inspect next by jointly considering the expectation and the variance of the predicted return through a mean-variance criterion. The mission environment models circular orbits with a spread in inclination and uses Edelbaum's combined-manoeuvre low-thrust transfer estimates. The observation comprises the orbital elements, the visit history, the observed risk state of each target and the remaining mission-time budget. The risk state of each target changes stochastically during the campaign, making the outcome of an inspection uncertain and creating operationally meaningful distinctions between risk-neutral and risk-averse planning strategies. Because the mission-time budget generally admits only a subset of the candidate targets, the planner must also select which targets to inspect. Numerical experiments on scenarios with five target satellites show that one risk-aversion weight moves the policy across a range of mean return and return variance, over an unchanged environment and reward. Risk-averse agents reduce the variance of the episodic return by approximately one half relative to risk-neutral agents and lower the probability of a mission loss, at a cost in mean return. To validate the learned policies, the efficient frontier, which gives the highest mean return attainable at each level of return variance, is computed exactly by enumerating the admissible visit orders. At equal variance, the learned policies lie close to this frontier in mean return, and the risk-averse policies have a lower probability of a mission loss than the frontier policies.

</details>


### [196] [Breaking Information Islands in Sparse Tuning via Small-World Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41665)
*Xianchao Guan,Zijun Xiong,Yifeng Wang,Yubo Cui,Yaowei Wang,Guo-Sen Xie,Xin Li,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为SWIFT的方法，该方法通过结合局部特征混合的块对角稀疏更新和固定确定性散列置换来解决稀疏调优的有效性问题，从而实现信息岛的减少。实验表明，SWIFT在常识推理、自然语言生成和图像分类等任务上优于参数高效微调基线，并可与全微调相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏调优方法可能由于支持结构的有限或不均匀连接导致信息岛问题，限制了特定任务间的直接交互。SWIFT方法通过引入小世界连接来打破这种局限。

Method: SWIFT方法结合了局部特征混合的块对角稀疏更新和无需额外可训练参数的固定确定性散列置换，以实现信息流动的优化。

Result: 理论结果证明了某些随机稀疏更新模型和块对角更新可能产生信息岛，而适当的散列置换能够保证正块级扩展。实验结果显示SWIFT在多种任务上优于参数高效微调基线，并有时甚至能够媲美全微调。

Conclusion: SWIFT由于其有效的稀疏调优策略而显示出更佳的性能，在保持少量参数更新的情况下实现了广泛的适用性和接近全微调的效果。

Abstract: Sparse tuning is widely used to adapt large language models due to its parameter efficiency. However, its effectiveness depends not only on the number of trainable parameters, but also on the feature-interaction topology of the sparse update's structural support. Limited or uneven connectivity in this support may isolate subsets of feature dimensions, forming information islands that restrict direct task-specific interactions through the trainable update. To address this issue, we propose Small-World Induced Fine-Tuning (SWIFT), a sparse tuning framework inspired by small-world connectivity. SWIFT combines block-diagonal sparse updates for local feature mixing with a fixed deterministic scattering permutation that routes the mixed outputs across feature groups without additional trainable parameters. We further show theoretically that certain random sparse-update models and block-diagonal updates can exhibit information islands, whereas a sufficiently scattering permutation guarantees positive block-level expansion when the trainable block-diagonal factor consists of equal-sized, structurally dense square blocks. Extensive experiments on commonsense reasoning, natural language generation, and image classification demonstrate that SWIFT outperforms the evaluated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) baselines on average and can match or surpass full fine-tuning. Implementation is available at https://github.com/xianchaoguan/SWIFT

</details>


### [197] [Radial Kernel Attention for Long-Range Modeling in Polymer Machine Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41675)
*Wenbo Fu,Chenghui Mao,Chengwen Hu,Haifeng Lv,Zhou Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RKA通过将原子距离映射到学习到的径向特征并结合其与内容相关的查询和键的重叠，实现了长范围建模的线性时间和可保守力的动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全局注意力机制（如全对注意力）会带来二次时间复杂度，不利于大规模聚合物体系的建模，RKA旨在降低计算复杂度并提高聚合物系统中的能量和力预测准确性。

Method: RKA首先将原子距离从簇质心映射到学习到的径向特征，然后结合这些特征与内容相关的查询和键。通过因子化的聚合和读出步骤，实现全局特征的线性通信成本。在局部块中插入不变的缩放更新，通过与总能量的径向坐标相关性进行微分来得到保守力。

Result: 我们的研究在OPoly26的有限聚合物衍生簇中证明，RKA模型的原子能量和力的平均绝对误差（MAE）分别减少了67.4%和36.3%。20M模型展现了优于UMA-S模型的性能，总能量MAE为15.81 meV，力MAE为2.619 meV/å，原子能量的MAE为0.1182 meV/原子。

Conclusion: RKA通过线性的时间复杂度实现长范围通信，提升了聚合物体系的能量和力预测性能，表明了其在大规模聚合物模型中的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Radial Kernel Attention (RKA) for long-range modeling in polymer machine learning force fields. Local neighbor graphs constrain communication across polymer chains and their surrounding environments, while explicit all-pair attention incurs quadratic cost. RKA maps atomic distances from the cluster centroid to learned radial features and combines their overlap with content-dependent queries and keys. Factorized aggregation and readout enable global feature exchange at linear cost in atom count for fixed feature dimensions. We insert the invariant scalar update between EquiformerV3 local blocks and obtain conservative forces by differentiating the total energy, including its radial coordinate dependence. We evaluate energy and force prediction for finite polymer-derived clusters in OPoly26. Our models are trained on the official training split for 12 epochs and evaluated directly after training on the complete official validation split. A matched 5M comparison shows that RKA reduces per-atom energy and force MAEs by 67.4% and 36.3%, respectively. The 20M RKA model is compared with UMA-S-1.2 and UMA-S-1.2.1 on the same validation structures. The 20M model attains a total-energy MAE of 15.81 meV and a force MAE of 2.619 meV/angstrom; its per-atom energy MAE is 0.1182 meV/atom. RKA provides a compact global communication pathway that improves energy and force prediction on the evaluated polymer-derived clusters.

</details>


### [198] [ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了ReuScope协议，用于审计数据再利用和评估敏感性，以Capsule Vision 2024案例研究为基础。通过改变评价人群和训练数据进行比较，发现删除特定训练样本后，公共验证集上的平衡准确性显著降低，但重新评估时又有所提升，表明该差异并非泄漏引起的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出了一种新的审计协议ReuScope，旨在评估和减少机器学习模型中由于数据再利用引起的敏感性问题，确保模型的稳健性和公平性。

Method: 对于Capsule Vision 2024案例，研究者通过调整训练数据和保持预测集不变以及调整评价集合和保持训练集不变两种方式来考核模型的敏感性。通过严格的实验设计，确保数据与模型之间的关系清晰可追溯。

Result: 实验结果显示，在删除特定训练样本后，公共验证集上的平衡准确性从82.5%降低到61.2%，但在重新评估时平衡准确性又提升了1.38个百分点。另外，通过对比分析发现，样本量和类别的数量无法解释观察到的得分差异。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，公共验证集的准确性降低是由于数据再利用的敏感性，而不是由于泄漏导致的性能提升。实验进一步揭示了模型在不同情况下的性能差异，并明确了这种差异的实际意义。

Abstract: We develop ReuScope, a protocol for auditing data reuse and evaluation sensitivity, using Capsule Vision 2024 as a case study. It combines measurements of data relationships with two comparisons: changing the evaluation population while holding predictions fixed, and changing training data while holding evaluation fixed. Byte-identical files cross the public training-validation boundary, and all 41 Kvasir-Capsule recordings that contribute frames occur in both partitions. Shared source recordings establish cross-split overlap; missing cross-recording patient identifiers prevent counting unique shared patients. Across ten paired seeds, removing all training rows attributed to Kvasir-Capsule, plus five rows from other sources flagged as near-matches to it, lowers public-validation balanced accuracy from 82.5% to 61.2%. Evaluating the same fitted models on the validation rows of the other sources reverses the comparison: removal improves balanced accuracy by 1.38 percentage points. The public-validation drop measures sensitivity to source removal, not an amount of leakage-induced inflation. Matched controls show that sample size and class counts alone do not explain the observed score differences. An exploratory experiment removes the training neighbors of validation frames that have close training matches, or equally many distant training rows, and yields a difference-in-differences estimate, relative to matched unflagged frames, of -0.355 percentage points in true-class probability; the paired-t interval for accuracy spans zero. The resulting procedure distinguishes observed reuse from conditional performance sensitivity and specifies the population, class support and uncertainty unit for each conclusion.

</details>


### [199] [Advancing Clinical Trial Outcome Prediction with Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41710)
*Burak Bakkar,Hana Moalla,Mohamed Salem Jedidi,Haifa Ben Messaoud,Nicolas Lopez Carranza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了BioBERT和BioMistral两种领域特定语言模型，用于临床试验阶段结果预测，改进了现有的HINT方法，使用预训练模型的探针方法在一些阶段上取得了与微调相当的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验失败是药物开发的主要障碍，增加了成本并延迟了患者接受有效治疗的时间。现有方法如HINT通过明确建模药物、疾病和方案之间的交互来预测临床试验结果，但不充分利用临床试验注册中的自由文本信息。

Method: 使用BioBERT（108M参数）和BioMistral（7B参数）两种领域特定语言模型，在TOP基准（12,477项试验）上进行阶段结果预测。将试验表示为从十个ClinicalTrials.gov字段构建的文本，比较了预训练模型的冻结和仅训练轻量分类器的方法。

Result: 所有四种语言模型变体在各个阶段的遗留测试集上都优于已发布的HINT结果，ROC-AUC和PR-AUC分别提高了0.613-0.772和0.649-0.901。F1差异较小且混合，没有模型明确超过始终预测试验成功的基准。在测试的配置下，探针与微调表现类似：微调模型在I和II期上获得了最高ROC-AUC和PR-AUC，冻结的BioMistral嵌入在III期上获得了最好的结果，尽管大多数变体之间的差异低于0.03且未进行显著性测试。

Conclusion: 预训练的生物医学表示已经包含了与试验结果相关的信息，探针方法比微调方法在该任务上更简便且效果相似，是这项任务的一个有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Clinical trial failures are a major barrier to efficient drug development, incurring substantial costs and delaying patient access to effective therapies. Structured approaches such as the Hierarchical Interaction Network (HINT) predict trial outcomes by explicitly modelling interactions between drugs, diseases and protocols, but make limited use of the free text in trial registrations. We evaluate two domain-specific language models, BioBERT (108M parameters) and BioMistral (7B parameters), for phase-level outcome prediction on the TOP benchmark (12,477 trials), representing each trial as text built from ten ClinicalTrials.gov fields. We compare fine-tuning (full fine-tuning for BioBERT, low-rank adaptation for BioMistral) with probing, in which the pretrained model is frozen and only a lightweight classifier is trained on its embeddings. On the held-out test set of each phase, all four language-model variants achieved higher ROC-AUC (0.613--0.772) and PR-AUC (0.649--0.901) than the published HINT results, corresponding to relative PR-AUC gains of 10--20\%. F1 differences were small and mixed, and no model, including HINT, clearly exceeded the F1 of a trivial classifier that predicts success for every trial. Under the configurations tested, probing performed comparably to fine-tuning: fine-tuned models scored highest on ROC-AUC and PR-AUC in Phases I and II, and frozen BioMistral embeddings in Phase III (ROC-AUC 0.772; PR-AUC 0.901), although most differences between variants were below 0.03 and were not tested for significance. These results suggest that pretrained biomedical representations already encode signal relevant to trial outcome, and that probing is a computationally cheaper alternative to fine-tuning for this task.

</details>


### [200] [Multimodal In-Context Learning with Explicit Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41754)
*Yuquan Li,Xinyang Jiang,Yuhao Xue,Cairong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的ERIM框架，通过显式地诱导和推理多模态学习中的潜在规则，改善了现有直接输入输出映射的方法，实验表明这种新方法在不同情境下表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理需要从演示中推导出潜在规则的任务时表现不佳，这限制了模型的性能和可解释性。ERIM框架旨在通过显式地诱导和推理这些规则来解决这一问题。

Method: ERIM框架利用多模态大语言模型的推理能力，构建以规则为中心的多模态学习数据集，引入明确定义的规则诱导监督，通过结合混合冷启动监督微调和强化学习的两阶段训练策略来微调多模态大语言模型，实现显式的规则诱导。

Result: ERIM在不同多模态在上下文学习场景中表现出一致的性能提升，直接诱导正确的潜在规则能显著提高模型的性能和稳定性。

Conclusion: ERIM框架为解决现有多模态在上下文学习中的问题提供了一个有效的解决方案，通过显式诱导和推理潜在规则，能够显著提升模型的性能和可解释性。

Abstract: A central challenge in multimodal in-context learning (MICL) arises from tasks that are ill-defined without context and require uncovering the underlying rules from demonstrations. Existing methods predominantly treat MICL as direct input-output mapping, overlooking the importance of explicitly inducing and reasoning over such rules, limiting both performance and interpretability. We propose ERIM, Explicit Rule-Induction for Multimodal In-Context Learning, a unified framework that enhances MICL by explicitly inducing the latent rules governing demonstration samples. Leveraging the reasoning ability of multi-modal large language model (MLLM), ERIM constructs rule-centric MICL datasets, introduces explicit supervision for rule induction, and fine-tunes MLLM to perform explicit rule-induction via a two-stage training strategy that combines mixed cold-start supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments show consistent performance improvements across diverse MICL settings and demonstrate that correctly inducing underlying rules directly leads to stronger and more stable performance.

</details>


### [201] [Clinical-Protocol-Aware Cyber Resilience for Connected Clinical Technologies Under AI-Enabled Adversarial Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41784)
*Pranav Patel,Siva Kannathasan,Prerak Patel,Jigar Patel,Vansh Patel,Sheel Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于临床协议感知的网络架构，通过微网络和可控互联实现临床环境下的网络弹性，实验表明该框架有助于降低网络安全暴露，并且提高了临床影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着医疗保健向连接的医疗设备、临床信息系统、云服务和分布式护理技术转型，网络安全风险日益增加。本文提出了一种结合临床因素的网络安全框架，旨在通过技术预防、隔离和响应措施来减轻这些风险，特别是在加速漏洞发现和攻击路径分析方面发挥重要作用。

Method: 该方法采用了参数化的蒙特卡洛模拟技术，区分了技术暴露与临床后果的模型。通过集成临床因素，评估了在连接架构、对手能力、安全控制、协议依赖、风险对齐、通信方向性、生命周期集成和响应时间等方面的绩效。

Result: 实验结果表明，连接架构相比分段架构增加了56.3%的技术暴露，而结合预防和隔离措施则可将泄露减少60.1%。增加协议依赖性显著提高了临床影响指数，同时技术暴露保持不变，说明技术和临床后果可能分离。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种临床语境感知的网络安全边界设计、控制优先级和协调隔离的方法，在连接的医疗环境中提供了针对性的网络安全解决方案。

Abstract: Healthcare increasingly relies on connected medical devices, clinical information systems, cloud services, and distributed-care technologies, creating cybersecurity risks across clinical workflows. AI-enabled capabilities can accelerate vulnerability discovery and attack-path analysis, increasing the need to limit compromise propagation. This paper proposes a clinical-protocol-aware cyber-resilience framework based on bounded clinical micro-networks and controlled interconnections. The framework integrates clinical factors into cybersecurity design, management, and evaluation, covering prevention, containment, and incident response. A parameterized Monte Carlo simulation distinguishes technical cyber exposure from modeled clinical consequence. Experiments investigate architectural connectivity, adversarial capability, security controls, protocol dependency, risk alignment, communication directionality, lifecycle integration, and response timing. Under the simulation assumptions, connected architecture increases mean cyber exposure by 56.3% relative to segmented architecture, while combined prevention and containment reduce exposure by 60.1% relative to no controls. Increasing protocol dependency raises the modeled clinical-impact index from 1.021 to 7.567 without changing mean cyber exposure, demonstrating that technical exposure and clinical consequence can diverge. Risk-alignment experiments further indicate higher modeled clinical impact when greater technical risk corresponds to greater clinical significance. The findings support comparative architectural analysis, not calibrated estimates of real-world cyber risk, control effectiveness, or patient harm. The framework provides a clinical-context-aware approach to security-boundary design, control prioritization, and coordinated containment in connected healthcare environments.

</details>


### [202] [Whose Values, Which Contexts, and What Signals? Grounding Cultural Value Alignment with Re3Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41793)
*Jing Yao,Hyunjin Hwang,Weihua Zheng,Bryan Chen Zhengyu Tan,Jia Wang Peh,Dylan Raharja,JinYeong Bak,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Rui Cao,Yuki Arase,Jacki O'Neill,Zhicheng Dou,Xing Xie,Xiaoyuan Yi,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过Re3Culture数据集探讨了文化价值对齐目标的构建方法，强调社区归属感、具体情境观察和明确的价值优先级提示的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管文化价值对齐需要模型优化，但如何将这些目标基于人类数据的具体化仍需探索。本文提出Re3Culture数据集以解决构建目标时面临的关键问题。

Method: 研究采用了代表性的社区（来自四个文化群体的700多名参与者）、基于社区指导的实际情境以及反映价值观的明确调查结果。通过这些内容，研究分析了文化价值观评估的重要性。

Result: 结果表明：1. 文化价值观估计需考虑社区归属和广泛内部覆盖；2. 需要在具体的社区导向情境中观察价值观；3. 需要明确地询问价值优先级，而不能仅仅依赖响应偏好或底线答案。

Conclusion: Re3Culture为文化价值观对齐的目标定义提供了第一个基于人类的数据资源，强调了实现这一目标的关键要求。

Abstract: Cultural value alignment requires not only model optimization but also a well-specified target grounded in real-human evidence. Yet how such targets should be grounded in human data remains largely unexplored, raising three fundamental questions: (1) whose values should be represented? (2) where should cultural values be observed? and (3) what signals reveal underlying values? We investigate these questions through Re3Culture, a large-scale dataset linking (i) Representative communities: 700+ human participants across four cultures recruited through cultural-affiliation screening and demographic-aware sampling; (ii) Realistic scenario: culturally sensitive scenarios selected by community members, e.g., family and kinship; and (iii) Revealed value signals: 1,400+ value surveys, 20,000+ topic-grounded behavioral principles and explicit value priorities with responses for 6,000+ situated questions. Our analysis shows why these choices matter for defining the target, with three implications. First, estimating cultural values requires both community affiliation and broad internal coverage. Substituting other cultures or restricting subgroup coverage shifts value estimates. LLM simulations also disproportionately reflect particular subgroups. Second, values must be observed in concrete, community-guided situations. Abstract questionnaire profiles weakly recover situated priorities, and communities differ in which scenarios they identify as culturally value-revealing. Third, value priorities should be elicited explicitly, not learned from response preferences or bottom-line answers alone. Preferences conflate value agreement with other response-level qualities, and similar answers can be derived from different value priorities. By addressing these requirements, Re3Culture provides the first human-grounded resource that explicitly informs target definition for cultural value alignment.

</details>


### [203] [RECAST: REconstruction of Chain-of-Thought via Auditable Structured Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41813)
*Qi Wang,Kang Xu,Chengcheng Wan,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了RECAST框架，用于从封闭模型中重建解释过程，而不依赖于隐藏的推理渠道，且框架已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，对大型闭源模型的解释过程通常是隐藏的，这限制了对模型推理过程的理解。文章提出了一种新的方法RECAST，旨在从这些模型中重建第一人称结构化解释，以提高模型的透明度。

Method: RECAST框架通过将解码器条件化于问题和目标模型的最终答案，恢复第一人称结构化的解释，对其进行清洗，并构建SFT（示例迁移学习）可用的数据。

Result: 研究人员成功地在一个前沿的闭源模型Claude-Sonnet-5.5上应用了RECAST，此外，还使用相同的“仅答案”协议在GPT-6.1和Gemini 3模型上实现了成功的人类解读结果。

Conclusion: RECAST展示了成为第一个能够从先进闭源模型中重建解释过程的系统，并以开源形式提供了一个完整的管道解决方案，以生成SFT可用的数据。

Abstract: Large closed-source reasoning models expose only their final answers. The intermediate reasoning that produces those answers is hidden behind API surfaces, tool-call policies, and safety filters. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) extraction methods assume a side channel: a visible reasoning field, a scratchpad carried through tool calls, or a signed/encrypted reasoning block. We reproduce three representative state-of-the-art extraction methods and find that, on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 accessed through the standard chat-completions interface, all three return zero CoT tokens. The failure is structural rather than a bug: the reasoning field is absent, named tool selection is rejected, free-text scratchpad arguments are filtered, and no replayable reasoning block is exposed. We therefore take a different route. We present RECAST, a reconstruction-based framework that conditions a decoder on the question and the target model's final answer, reconstructs a first-person structured rationale, cleans it, and builds SFT-ready data. To the best of our knowledge, RECAST is the first system to reconstruct CoT from frontier closed models such as Claude-Sonnet-5.5, GPT-6.1, and Gemini 3 without using hidden reasoning channels. We instantiate RECAST on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 and additionally obtain successful qualitative reconstructions on GPT-6.1 and Gemini 3 with the same answer-only protocol. The system is open-source and packaged as an agent skill: one prompt deploys it and runs the complete pipeline to produce SFT-ready CoT data. This preprint describes the system, its threat model, and a controlled artifact validation; it does not claim large-scale benchmark improvements.

</details>


### [204] [Dynamical Memory Transformer: Selective Recurrent Memories with Constant Cost Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41850)
*Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Dynamical Memory Transformer (DMT)模型，结合了Transformer的长序列记忆能力和Dynamical系统的信息检索特性，实现了固定大小的递归状态和卷积缓存，展示了在认知任务和语言模型任务上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer模型虽然能够保留序列历史，但随着上下文长度增加，KV-Cache的大小和新token的成本也随之增加，State Space Models虽然压缩了过去的记忆但减少了可访问性。因此，作者提出Dynamical Memory Transformer来解决这些问题。

Method: DMT模型通过分离序列编码和信息读取，使用多个由复杂对角递归矩阵组成的动态系统实现记忆单元，输入依赖的泄漏率控制其更新进度。解码器则使用注意力机制直接读取并组合这些记忆单元的结果，而无需遍历token的历史。

Result: DMT模型在多项认知任务和语言建模任务上表现出色，尤其是在选择性复制任务上表现出更强的长度外推能力，其性能与标准Transformer相近，但在语言建模任务上仍略逊于Mamba。

Conclusion: DMT模型展示了并行动态记忆单元在保留和使用序列信息方面的潜力，是一个有前景的信息保留和使用机制。

Abstract: Transformers retain an explicit history of the sequence, allowing attention to directly retrieve past representations. However, the size of the KV-Cache and the cost of a new token increase with the context length. State Space Models compress the past into a fixed-size recurrent state, at the cost of reduced addressability. We present the Dynamical Memory Transformer (DMT), a hybrid architecture that separates sequence encoding from information reading. The encoder uses several memory units, each implemented by a dynamical system with a complex diagonal recurrent matrix. An input-dependent leak rate controls their updates. The decoder then uses attention to read and combine their outputs, without going through the token history. The formulation of the recurrence allows an associative scan with O(T ) total work and O(log T ) parallel depth, with fixed dimensions. During autoregressive inference, the recurrent states and the convolution cache remain fixed in size. We compare DMT with Mamba and Transformer on cognitive and language modeling tasks, and show its strengths and limitations: DMT achieves the best results on several cognitive tasks and language modeling performance close to that of Transformer, but remains behind Mamba in language modeling. On selective copy, DMT also shows stronger length extrapolation than Mamba and Transformer. These results demonstrate that parallel dynamical memory units are a promising mechanism for retaining and using information over sequences.

</details>


### [205] [Learning to Interact and Direct: Co-Evolving Video Director Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41865)
*Yingzhao Jian,Zhongan Wang,Yuxuan Hou,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了LIT框架，结合了导演代理、模拟用户和元工程师，通过共同进化提高导演和交互能力，并在测试集和OOD测试集上分别提高了生产和交互得分19.1%和32.0%，但仍存在依赖于元代理被动归因的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的导演代理在自进化叙事规划方面有所进展，但导演和用户交互之间的相互强化尚未得到充分关注。增强交互可以澄清用户意图并指导工作流程的优化，而可靠的执行则可使合作从问题解决转向创造性决策。

Method: LIT框架包含导演代理、模拟用户和元工程师三个组成部分：模拟用户负责分配创意任务以揭示导演的弱点，而元工程师则利用任务记录来改进导演的交互和生产策略。为了克服仅依赖于元代理被动归因的问题，引入共同进化机制让元代理主动学习外部知识，从而提高问题发现和解决方案建议的能力。

Result: LIT在测试集和OOD测试集上分别提高了生产和交互得分19.1%和32.0%，并且元代理、模拟用户和导演形成了一个进化的闭环，共同增强了系统的导演和交互能力。

Conclusion: LIT框架通过共同进化有效提高了导演和交互能力，但其适应更广泛的用户交互模式和生产技术的能力仍受到限制。

Abstract: Video director agents orchestrate multistage production to realize users' creative visions. Emerging research on their self-evolution advances screenplay planning effectively. However, the mutual reinforcement between directing and user interaction remains largely overlooked. Improved interaction clarifies user intent and informs workflow refinement, while more reliable execution shifts collaboration from troubleshooting to creative decisions. To this end, we introduce LIT (Learning to Interact and DirecT), a framework comprises a director agent, a simulated user, and a meta engineer. Simulated user assigns creative tasks to probe the director's weaknesses, while the meta engineer uses the task traces to revise the director's interaction and production strategy. Although this approach can address discovered interaction and production issues, it still relies on passive attribution by the meta agent. This limits its ability to adapt to broader user interaction patterns and production techniques. To solve this problem, we equip LIT with a co-evolution paradigm. The meta agent proactively learns external knowledge, thereby improving its ability to discover problems and propose solutions. The meta agent, simulated user, and director together form an evolutionary closed loop, collaboratively enhances the system's directing and interaction capabilities. Across the Test and OOD Test splits, LIT improves production and Interaction scores by 19.1% and 32.0%, respectively, relative to the initial pipeline. Controlled ablations reveal the correlation between improvements in production and interaction, where the co-evolution mechanism further encourage directing skill and interaction mode exploration.

</details>


### [206] [Fuzzy selective state model for forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41879)
*P. A. Golovinski,A. A. Drobyshev,A. O. Shatalova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章提出了用于预测零售连锁客户的消费者偏好动态的方法，采用一种受Mamba型选择性状态空间模型启发的模型，考虑了竞争促销和客户切换的影响，实验表明该模型在预测中心的MAE为0.154，优于常惯性演化算子和朴素预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过精确建模消费者偏好动态，帮助企业更好地理解和预测客户行为变化，尤其是在市场竞争加剧的情况下。

Method: 模型采用Mamba型选择性状态空间模型，隐状态为三角模糊数，通过M.G. Matveev提出的双分量模糊数的代数进行计算。参数计算基于明确特征，而模型的模糊部分保持线性且非负。

Result: 模型在合成数据上的实验显示，与常惯性演化算子和朴素预测相比，其预测中心的平均绝对误差更低，提供了一种可解释的模糊度估计，且能捕捉客户永久转向竞争者的迹象。

Conclusion: 研究表明，该模型能够有效预测消费者的偏好变化，同时保持预测的清晰度和解释性，为企业的决策提供了有力支持。

Abstract: We consider forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences of retail chain customers. A customer's preference is described by a triangular fuzzy number. The evolution of the preference over time is governed by a Mamba-type selective state space model whose hidden state is a triangular number, with computations carried out in M.G.~Matveev's two-component algebra of fuzzy numbers. The recurrence parameters are computed from crisp features, while the fuzzy part of the model remains linear with non-negative coefficients. We show that the state then always remains a proper triangular number and is a convex combination of past observations, so that the fuzziness of the forecast does not accumulate over time and the algebraic computations coincide with Zadeh's extension principle. On a synthetic example with competitor promotions and customer switching, a lightweight 107-parameter model reaches a mean absolute error of the forecast centre of 0.154 versus 0.163 for the constant-inertia evolution operator and 0.179 for the naive forecast, matches a crisp model of the same architecture and additionally provides a linguistically interpretable estimate of forecast vagueness. We also show how the linguistic readout of the forecast can be used to detect a permanent switch of a customer to a competitor.

</details>


### [207] [Automated Keyword Generation Using LLMs in a High-Volume Data Virtualization Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41893)
*Saiful Khan,Yiwen Xing,Joyraj Chakraborty,Afrah Farea,Philip Beaucamp,Samuel Jackson,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个使用大语言模型（LLM）为虚拟数据集生成关键词的解决方案，通过多种策略处理不同文本数据，并实验证明生成关键词的性能依赖于文本描述的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据虚拟化服务（DVS）允许用户为不同的处理任务创建源数据集的多个版本，但需要一个有效的搜索引擎来帮助不同用户发现现有的虚拟数据集，以避免重复努力。

Method: 提出了五种不同的文本数据选择策略，并将它们应用于五个应用程序案例研究中生成的虚拟数据集。使用了三种不同的LLM生成器和两种嵌入技术进行实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，生成关键词的性能取决于文本描述的质量。这有助于采用多种策略构建一个新的关键词生成服务（KGS）作为扩展DVS的一部分。

Conclusion: 研究证明，自动关键词生成能力对于高数据量和频繁访问的DVS来说是必不可少的。

Abstract: In modern data infrastructure, a data virtualization service (DVS) allows users to create many versions of source datasets for different processing tasks. For example, a multivariate or multi-modality dataset can be used for different machine learning tasks (e.g., classification, detection, segmentation, forecasting, and so on). Data selection, participation, integration, and preprocessing in different tasks result in many virtual datasets [Khan et al., SSC 2026]. Meanwhile, different users need to discover existing virtual datasets frequently and easily to avoid duplicate efforts. To enable an effective search engine in a DVS, it is desirable to automatically generate keywords for each virtual dataset. In this work, we present a solution for generating keywords for virtual datasets using large language models (LLMs). We propose five strategies for selecting different collections of text data for processing by the LLMs. We applied the five strategies to virtual datasets created in five application case studies with different source datasets. We conducted experiments with three LLM generators and two embedding techniques. The experiments show that the performances of the five strategies depend on the quality of the text descriptions. The results help us adopt a multi-strategy implementation of a new keyword generation service (KGS) as part of an extended DVS. The work demonstrates that the capability of automated keyword generation is indispensable for a high-volume DVS that is dataset-rich and frequently accessed.

</details>


### [208] [Using Machine Learning to Measure Self-Efficacy Belief Through Learners' Interaction Traces in Technology-Enhanced Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41900)
*Maeva Somny,Linda Chouati,Marie Lefevre*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究旨在通过数据挖掘和机器学习技术识别学习者自我效能感低的特征，以改善预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技术增强学习（TEL）环境在支持学习表现和动机方面显示出强大潜力，探讨自我效能感在这些环境中的作用变得尤为重要。

Method: 研究通过实验收集了两所大学的学生交互轨迹和自我效能感的自我报告量表，开发了包含多个分类模型的机器学习流程。

Result: 研究发现，密切监测学习者的自我效能感对于提高预测性能非常重要，因为学习过程中自我效能感具有高度动态性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，早期检测自我效能感低可以帮助实施补救措施，支持学生的自我效能感发展。

Abstract: Self-efficacy beliefs play a critical role in learners' performance, academic choices, and, more broadly, in their future career trajectories. As Technology-Enhanced Learning (TEL) environments have demonstrated strong potential for supporting both performance and motivation, investigating self-efficacy within such environments is of particular interest, especially for implementing remedial practices as early as possible to support learners' self-efficacy beliefs. Our research aims to develop a system capable of detecting low self-efficacy based on learners' interaction traces while they solve exercises in a TEL system. In this paper, we present initial results and future perspectives on a detection approach based on data mining and machine learning techniques to identify features indicative of low self-efficacy beliefs. Because the detection of learners' self-efficacy beliefs through their use of TEL systems remains largely unexplored, this work is exploratory in nature. To this end, we conducted an experiment across two universities to collect interaction traces and self-reported measures of self-efficacy. We then developed a machine learning pipeline comprising multiple classification models, enabling us to highlight the importance of closely monitoring of learners' self-efficacy beliefs for improved prediction performance, given their highly dynamic nature during learning.

</details>


### [209] [ReX-GG: a LLM Ensemble Pipeline for Relation-extraction and Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41901)
*Giacomo Magnifico,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种定制的轻量级大型语言模型联合工作流，通过使用JSON结构化输出和匿名同行评审排名来减少幻觉输出，并提高模型鲁棒性，适用于关系抽取任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM集成框架通常需要多步骤设置，并添加额外的模块进行答案排序，导致复杂的架构和潜在的管道故障点。这些框架可能在更复杂的管道中缺乏输出控制，并且更快更轻的解决方案更容易传播幻觉。因此，作者提出了一个更轻的解决方案来处理这些缺陷。

Method: 该方法利用大语言模型的协调工作流来处理JSON结构化输出和匿名同行评审排名，以减少幻觉输出。通过在流行科学文章上应用的关系抽取任务来实现这一目标，该任务使用了四种知识引导的关系类型（强因果关系、弱因果关系、对比关系和组成关系），最终生成带有语义节点规范化和交互式彩色HTML因果图的时间输出。

Result: 该框架在一项用户研究中实现了0.778的平均感知准确率，超过了人类注释的标准。该研究还展示了该架构支持灵活部署以适应不同的LLM设置，并且整个框架在开放许可下发布以促进可重复性和合作研究。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过设计一种轻量级的工作流来改进LLM集成，通过使用JSON结构化输出和匿名同行评审排名来降低幻觉输出，提高了性能和架构的灵活性，促进了后续研究。

Abstract: Current LLM ensemble frameworks focus on multi-step setups with additional modules for answer ranking, often opting for token and span analysis rather than structured outputs, leading to heavyweight architectures with potential fail states along the pipeline. Faster, lighter solutions are more vulnerable to hallucination propagation and can lack output control in more complex pipelines. This paper proposes a customisable, lightweight ensemble workflow of coordinated Large Language Models that leverages JSON-structured outputs and anonymous peer-review ranking to mitigate hallucinatory outputs and single-model failure points. The pipeline is demonstrated on a relation extraction task applied to popular science articles in English, targeting four ontologically-grounded relation types (strong causation, weak causation, contrastive, and compositional), with semantic node canonicalisation and interactive, colour-coded HTML causal graphs as the final output. Performance is evaluated through an anonymous user study, achieving an average perceived accuracy of 0.778 against a human-annotated gold standard. The modular architecture supports flexible deployment across both API-based and in-house LLM setups, and the full framework is released under an open license to foster reproducibility and collaborative research.

</details>


### [210] [Structure-Aware Skill Retrieval and Arbitration for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41909)
*Anran Liu,Benzhao Hou,Zongsheng Cao,Daichao Wang,Lang Chen,Shitong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ArchOR 提出了一种基于原型中心的方式，通过两种检索优化模块 (CARL 和 S³R) 改进了技能抽取与应用策略，在五个基准测试上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语言模型在面对叙事变化时表现不佳，且经验存储在实例层面，不够灵活。为了解决这些问题，作者提出了 ArchOR，旨在通过增强技能抽取和检索策略来提高模型的鲁棒性和重用性。

Method: ArchOR 引入了两个关键模块：CARL 和 S³R。CARL 通过对缓存的技能表示进行原型感知的细化来优化顺序。S³R 为技能附加开放式结构签名，并通过加权的 Jaccard 重叠度优先选择候选者，同时限制语义重排仅限于 top-K 候选集合。最终，通过仲裁规则决定是否采用结构性路径来替换原有的选择器。

Result: ArchOR 使用与 OptSkills 相同的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 模型和技能库，在五个基准测试中达到了65.00% 的 Micro-Avg Pass@1，相比 OptSkills 提升了1.64 个百分点在 Micro-Avg 和2.68 个百分点在 Macro-Avg。

Conclusion: ArchOR 通过增强技能检索机制，实现了更好的表现与鲁棒性，并展示了其在多个基准测试中的卓越能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can turn natural-language optimization tasks into solver-checked answers, but current agents remain brittle under narrative rewrites and often store reusable experience at the instance level. We introduce \textsc{ArchOR}, an archetype-centric skill agent that builds on \textsc{OptSkills} by adding two retrieval-focused modules. \emph{Centroid-Aware Representation Learning} (CARL) refines the ordering of cached skill representations through a low-rank prototype residual. \emph{Signature-Guided Retrieval} (S\textsuperscript{3}R) attaches open-dimensional structural signatures to skills, prioritizes candidates by weighted Jaccard overlap, and bounds semantic re-ranking to a top-$K$ set. A baseline-preserving arbitration rule determines when the structural route should replace the original selector. With the same DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone and released skill library, \textsc{ArchOR} reaches 65.00\% Micro-Avg.\ Pass@1 across five benchmarks, improving over \textsc{OptSkills} by 1.64~pp Micro-Avg.\ and 2.68~pp Macro-Avg.

</details>


### [211] [Sensorium: An Open-Source Multimodal Data Collection and Curation Library for Embodied Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41946)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Ariel Rodriguez,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Embodied surgical AI requires reliable multimodal data, yet surgical data collection remains costly, difficult to repeat, and strongly dependent on the hardware setup. We present Sensorium, an open-source Python library for recording, processing, annotating, and curating multimodal surgical data from configurable robot and sensor streams using the Robot Operating System (ROS) and ROS2 middleware. The library combines operator-facing data acquisition, configurable processing, semantic annotation, and episodic dataset generation. We evaluate Sensorium across 1516 recordings of phantom, ex vivo, and in vivo porcine experiments involving multiple surgical tasks and sensing configurations, and report preliminary usability feedback.

</details>


### [212] [Optimizing Stochastic Policy Geometry for Few-Step Flow Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41949)
*Zhuguanyu Wu,Congjia Chen,Ruihao Gong,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Yi Xu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Geo-FSRL引入了一种优化方案，通过最小化给定非零速度输出更新下的条件Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度，选择允许的策略，实现了在同质高斯转换族下的唯一闭合形式优化器，适用于所有非零更新幅度和方向。该方法还采用梯度尺度匹配，提供了闭合形式的策略梯度和KL权重。Geo-FSRL在图像和视频生成模型的4步设置中，实现了更高的最终奖励分数，相比Flow-GRPO和Flow-CPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决应用GRPO（基于图像生成的端到端建模方法）到少数步骤流模型时遇到的政策选择问题，特别是当单个转换覆盖广泛的噪声级别范围时，现有的解决方案需要引入随机性并通常受到目标流系数保真度约束，但这些约束并未提供一种选择策略的准则。Geo-FSRL通过最小化基于更新量和方向的条件KL散度，提供了一个唯一的优化策略选择方法，从而在不牺牲模型灵活性的情况下改善了优化结果。

Method: Geo-FSRL构建在选择原则之上：在固定的条件状态和时间步骤配对中，选择一个最小化任何给定的非零速度输出更新下的条件KL散度的可接受策略。在同质高斯转换族中，这个准则简化为在每次非零更新下都最小化一个单一的几何因子，该因子决定了每次更新的条件KL散度。该方法还采用了梯度尺度匹配，推导出闭合形式的策略梯度和KL权重，以标准化明确的分数尺度和引用-KL曲率。最终，Geo-FSRL在不改变原始少数步骤计划和保留推断中的确定性解决器的情况下进行训练。

Result: 在4步设置下，Geo-FSRL在图像和视频生成模型上的最终奖励分数都超过了Flow-GRPO和Flow-CPS，同时保持了与原始少数步骤计划一致的训练过程和基于确定性的推断解决方案。

Conclusion: Geo-FSRL提供了一种有效的策略优化方法，它在少数步骤流模型中成功地实现了高效率和高性能，特别是在图像和视频生成任务上，Geo-FSRL优于现有的方法，证明了其在深度生成模型优化方面的潜力和创新性。

Abstract: Few-step flow models generate samples through a handful of large denoising transitions. Applying GRPO to such models requires introducing stochasticity into the denoising transitions, and when a single transition spans a wide range of noise levels, the choice of the resulting stochastic policy becomes a major factor in optimization. Recent constructions constrain the policy to preserve the target flow coefficients, but this constraint admits a family of policies and offers no criterion for choosing among them. We introduce Geo-FSRL (Geometry-Optimized Few-Step Reinforcement Learning), built on a selection principle: at a fixed conditioning state and timestep pair, choose the admissible policy that minimizes the conditional KL divergence induced by any given nonzero velocity-output update. Within a coefficient-preserving family of isotropic Gaussian transitions, this criterion reduces to minimizing a single scalar geometry factor that determines the conditional KL for every velocity-output update. The same factor governs velocity-space Fisher information and conditional transition log-likelihood-ratio variance. We derive the unique closed-form optimizer within this family, shared across all nonzero update magnitudes and directions, with transition coefficients determined by both the source and target noise levels. Because the minimized geometry factor still varies across timesteps, we further adopt gradient-scale matching from prior work and derive closed-form policy-gradient and KL weights that normalize explicit score scales and reference-KL curvature. Geo-FSRL trains on the original few-step schedule and retains the deterministic solver at inference. Our method achieves higher final reward scores than Flow-GRPO and Flow-CPS on both image generation and video generation models under the 4-step setting. Code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/lightx2v_rl

</details>


### [213] [Prune Gradually, Adapt Sparsely: Efficient Pruning of Large Language Models at Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41968)
*Jean Lucien Randrianantenaina,Hung Nguyen,Nasib Ullah,Jinbin Zhang,Nilesh Gupta,Inderjit S. Dhillon,Rohit Babbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ETAPS 提出了一种新的剪枝方法，通过逐渐减少稀疏等级并在每次剪枝步骤中使用固定预算的稀疏适应来适应剪枝后的模型，从而在 50% 到 90% 的稀疏性范围内的六种不同规模的模型中实现了比 ELSA 更低的困惑度，并且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的剪枝方法难以在剪枝和保持质量之间找到平衡，尤其是在极高稀疏性和较低稀疏成本之间。

Method: ETAPS 提出了一种新的剪枝方法，通过逐渐减少稀疏等级并在每次剪枝步骤中使用固定预算的稀疏适应来适应剪枝后的模型。

Result: ETAPS 在 50% 到 90% 的稀疏性范围内的六种不同规模的模型中实现了比 ELSA 更低的困惑度，并且成本更低。

Conclusion: ETAPS 提供了一种在保持高质量的同时减少剪枝成本的方法，尤其是在极高稀疏性的场景下。

Abstract: Weight pruning can reduce the storage and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), but preserving quality at high sparsity levels remains challenging. Existing methods face a quality-cost trade-off in this regime. One-shot pruning is cheap but degrades sharply as sparsity increases, even when followed by post-pruning recovery. A growing line of work instead optimizes the mask directly from the dense model under the end-to-end objective; among these methods, [Lee et al.,2026], the recent state of the art at extreme sparsity, reaches high quality but at the cost of model-sized optimizer state and auxiliary variables. To address this gap, we propose ETAPS, which prunes gradually and adapts sparsely: it tightens a global sparsity constraint in progressive stages while training a sparse delta with a small, fixed budget. Our key observation is that gradual weight removal keeps each pruning step small enough for sparse adaptation to compensate, allowing the adapted model to provide end-to-end gradients that guide the next pruning decision without model-sized optimizer state. Across six models from 125M to 13B parameters, ETAPS achieves lower perplexity than ELSA at every evaluated sparsity from 50% to 90%, with perplexity reductions of up to 42%. ETAPS is also far cheaper: at 90% sparsity, it prunes LLaMA2-7B on a single H200, where ELSA uses four, with $5.0\times$ smaller total persistent state and 49% fewer GPU-hours.
